AI agents and custom GenAI workflows are creating new, hidden ways for sensitive enterprise data to leak —and most teams don't even realize it.  If you're building, deploying, or managing AI systems, now is the time to ask: Are your AI agents exposing confidential data without your knowledge?  Most GenAI models don't intentionally leak data. But with their rise comes a subtle, dangerous assumption: that the convenience of SaaS extends to resilience. It doesn't. These platforms weren't built with full-scale data protection in mind .
